Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any discrete_math module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 125. The projective resolution has length at most 935 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the discrete_math space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 125 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 935. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the discrete_math object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 685. The moduli space has dimension at most 336 with 3 connected components.

For the inductive step, we decompose the discrete_math structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 336. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
